# Uneed
> Uneed is a curated product discovery platform with 10,000+ digital tools ranked by community votes. Makers launch products to get visibility through daily rankings, weekly/monthly/yearly awards, and expert reviews. Users discover the best tools across AI, marketing, design, development, and more.
## Documentation Sets
- [Full Documentation](https://www.uneed.best/llms-full.txt): Complete product directory with detailed descriptions, expert reviews, and alternative comparisons
- [All Products Directory](https://www.uneed.best/llms/all-products.txt): Complete directory of all 10,000 products grouped by category
## MCP Server & API (for AI agents)
Uneed exposes its product data to AI agents through a free, read-only remote MCP server and a REST API. Available tools: search_products, get_product, get_alternatives, get_reviews, get_trending, get_recent_launches, list_deals, list_products_for_sale, list_categories, get_launch_stats.
- [MCP endpoint](https://mcp.uneed.best/mcp): Remote MCP server (Streamable HTTP, no auth required)
- [REST API](https://mcp.uneed.best/v1/search?q=example): Same data over plain GET endpoints
- [OpenAPI spec](https://mcp.uneed.best/openapi.json): Machine-readable description of the REST endpoints
- [Monthly launch statistics](https://mcp.uneed.best/v1/stats/launches): Open data (CC BY 4.0, credit "Source: Uneed, uneed.best"), products submitted, declined and launched on Uneed per month, documented at https://www.uneed.best/mcp#launch-stats
## Launch a product (for AI agents)
AI agents can submit and schedule a product launch on Uneed on a user's behalf, end-to-end without a browser (email OTP auth, then either the free waiting line, with a date assigned automatically at least 50 days out and a launch-day upvote score of 10 (upvotes weighted by each voter's multiplier) needed to stay published and 20 to keep a do-follow link, or a paid Skip-the-Waiting-Line launch on a chosen date).
- [Agent Launch Guide](https://www.uneed.best/launch.txt): Step-by-step guide + REST API (/api/v1) to launch a product as an agent
## How Uneed Works
Makers submit products, the community votes daily, and top products earn weekly/monthly/yearly awards. Uneed also publishes expert reviews and curated alternatives.
- [Submit a Tool](https://www.uneed.best/submit-a-tool): Submit your product to get featured on Uneed
- [Daily Rankings](https://www.uneed.best/): Products ranked by daily community votes
- [Weekly Winners](https://www.uneed.best/weekly): Best products of the week by votes
- [Monthly Winners](https://www.uneed.best/monthly): Best products of the month
- [Yearly Winners](https://www.uneed.best/yearly): Best products of the year
- [Pricing](https://www.uneed.best/pricing): Launch and promotion options for makers
